Cross-strategy LOO d1-d3 accuracy (generalization.tex L95)\\n- **45.9%** (mean across 9 folds)\\n- Source: confusion matrices in `w6_cross_strategy_per_class_recall.json`\\n- Method: (d1_correct + d2_correct + d3_correct) / 15000 per fold, then averaged\\n- Note: 4-class mean was 59.1%; d0's near-perfect accuracy inflates it. Excluding d0 drops to 45.9%.\\n- Range across folds: 35.4% (pythia_topk50) to 59.1% (pythia_nucleus)\\n\\n### Artifact\\n- `artifacts/placeholder_values.json` — full breakdown with per-fold numbers\\n\\n## Downstream Curation PoC Insights (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### Oracle ≤ Unfiltered（反直觉发现）\\n- 完美 depth 筛选（oracle）反而不优于 unfiltered 全量数据：oracle_strict PPL 15.879 vs unfiltered 15.776（p=0.22）\\n- 原因推测：纯按 depth 筛选过度移除了有用的多样性，RF 的分类误差反而保留了有价值的变异\\n- **Framing 含义**：不能 claim \\\"RF approximates oracle\\\"，应 claim \\\"data efficiency\\\"（RF 用 44% 数据 ≈ 全量 PPL）\\n\\n### rf_moderate 统计检验偏差\\n- 我们的 t=-3.77 (p=0.02) vs Reviewer 手算 t=-5.58 (p=0.005)\\n- 其余 3 组对比偏差 <8%，此组偏差较大，以原始数据直接计算为准\\n- 定性结论一致（均显著），不影响 claim\\n\\n## Wild Data Calibration 跨数据集一致性 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n- Web-crawl FPR ~20% 是跨数据集一致的现象（C4=20.2%, FineWeb=19.5%, Δ<1pp），由模板/SEO 内容驱动\\n- 编辑质量文本（WikiText）FPR 稳定 0%\\n- 所有 web-crawl 误分类均为 d1（无 d2/d3 false positives），误分类程度温和\\n- AutoDL 代理下 HF streaming/大文件下载不可靠，改用 hf-mirror.com 下载 parquet 可绕过（FineWeb 2GB/114s）\\n\\n## LLM Zero-Shot Failure Modes (2026-05-16)\\n\\n- 两个 LLM（DeepSeek-v3.2 36.2%, Qwen2.5-7B 30.4%）均远低于 RF 78.2%，验证 zero-shot 一般性劣势\\n- 不同 LLM 的 failure mode 截然不同：DeepSeek 几乎全猜 d0（recall 95.2%），Qwen 偏向 d1（recall 55.2%）但 d0 recall 仅 26.4%\\n- 共同点：都无法捕获深层递归信号（d2/d3 recall 均很低）\\n- 论文叙事：两个模型的不同 failure pattern 说明这不是 prompt 偏差问题，而是 LLM zero-shot 根本无法访问 depth 所需的统计特征（surprisal distribution）\\n\\n## Multi-τ Ablation 关键洞察 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Memorization-as-prerequisite 机制\\n- τ=1.1 崩溃的根因不是\\\"特征变了\\\"而是\\\"特征消失了\\\"：高温使 token 分布过于随机，模型无法学习自身输出模式，depth-dependent 的 memorization 效应不再发生\\n- 训练 loss 是直接证据：τ=0.5 时 loss 2.27→0.42→0.16（快速 memorization），τ=1.1 时 loss 2.27→1.91→1.85（几乎不降）\\n- **论文含义**：depth signal 的存在前提是递归训练会改变模型——当温度太高时训练数据等同于随机噪声，模型不变，depth 无可探测\\n\\n### τ 鲁棒性范围\\n- τ ∈ [0.5, 0.9] 均 d1v2 AUC > 0.80，depth signal 不是单一温度伪影\\n- τ=0.7 是 d1v2 AUC 峰值（0.851），接近 nucleus p=0.95 的 0.861\\n- 这回答了 Reviewer R2-W3 的关切：depth signal 跨温度鲁棒，但有上界\\n\\n### Cross-τ 不可迁移性\\n- Cross-τ LOO mean acc=46.7%，与 cross-strategy LOO (59.1%) 一致但更差\\n- 原因：不同 τ 产生不同的 surprisal distribution 形状，feature space 不可直接对齐\\n- 与论文现有 \\\"strategy-dependent feature space\\\" 叙事一致，multi-τ 提供了同一 strategy（temperature）内部的细粒度证据\\n\\n## Instruction-Tuning 正则化发现 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### 核心发现（D032）\\n- 原假设错误：以为 IT 引入 U-shape → 实际 BASE 本身就有 U-shape\\n- 正确解读：IT **正则化** depth signal（使 AUC 单调递减），而非引入伪影\\n- 证据对比：BASE AUC d2v3=0.6815 (dip) → d3v4=0.73 (recovery) = U-shape; Instruct AUC 单调递减 0.949→0.877→0.771→0.716→0.618\\n- **论文含义**：IT 不是 confound 而是有利条件，移除了关于\\\"所有实验用 IT 模型是否引入系统偏差\\\"的审稿顾虑\\n\\n### EDD Bug 教训\\n- `llama3_eval.py` 硬编码阈值 0.55 而非项目标准 0.70，导致初始 EDD 报告为 \\\">5\\\" 而非正确的 EDD=3\\n- **规则**：任何 eval 脚本中的 EDD 阈值必须参数化并默认 0.70，不得硬编码\\n- 影响范围：仅 BASE 实验的 eval 路径受影响；cross_scale_llama3_8b_003 使用了正确阈值（验证通过）\\n\", \"total_chars\": 3309, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — recursive_gen_depth_est\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 25, 2026)\\n- Backup: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, full paper August 1, 2026)\\n- EMNLP 2026 special theme \\\"New Missions for NLP Research\\\" explicitly calls for work on data contamination, synthetic data feedback loops, and data governance — direct venue fit\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条 + Discussion implication)\\n\\n1. **Ordinal depth estimation is feasible, accurate, and robust across scales and generation modes**: Given text alone, recursive generation depth (d ∈ {0,1,2,...,K}) can be estimated with high accuracy using statistical features from reference-model perplexity distributions. On our 3-model × 3-strategy benchmark (1–1.5B), Random Forest classifiers on 19 statistical features achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy (chance=25%), with the best configuration (GPT-2 XL, temp τ=0.9) reaching 85.4%. Feature-based methods (RF, CORAL, DivEye) consistently outperform single-feature baselines across all settings. **Scale robustness**: Clean FP16 13B experiment (Qwen2.5-14B, no quantization) confirms depth signal persists at mainstream model scale, addressing the \\\"small model only\\\" concern from prior 7B QLoRA experiments. **Ecological validity**: Prompted generation experiment (topic-seeded text, not BOS-only unconditional) confirms depth signal is not an artifact of unnatural generation mode.\\n\\n2. **Depth Degradation Atlas — systematic characterization of recursive collapse** (PRIMARY): We provide the first systematic empirical characterization of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive generation depths, revealing (a) perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl) and surprisal variance (var_surprisal) as the dominant depth signal family — consistently occupying the top-4 features across all models and strategies, though their relative ranking is model-dependent, (b) non-uniform inter-depth distances (depth 0→1 gap >> depth 2→3 gap), (c) an Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable, and (d) **strategy-dependent feature trajectory reversal** — temperature sampling causes the depth-1→depth-2 feature boundary to invert relative to nucleus/top-k (cross-strategy LOO d1v2 AUC as low as 0.13), demonstrating that decoding strategy fundamentally shapes the degradation trajectory rather than merely attenuating it, and (e) **accelerated convergence mechanism** — root cause analysis confirms a genuine structural distributional shift: temperature's uniform logit scaling (vs nucleus/top-k truncation) accelerates convergence to the attractor distribution, so temp09 depth N features ≈ nucleus/topk50 depth N+1 (GPT-2 XL temp09 d1 p90_ppl median=92.2, falls into nucleus/topk50 d2 range 65–92). This is not a simple scale artifact fixable by normalization — z-score normalization improves overall LR cross-strategy accuracy (25%→61%) but does **not** recover pairwise d1v2 AUC (GPT-2 XL 0.50→0.24, OLMo 0.50→0.19), and does **not** improve tree-based classifiers (RF Δ<0.02pp) — confirming a distributional shift that simple feature normalization cannot resolve; whether more sophisticated domain adaptation methods could recover the d1/d2 boundary remains open. This atlas spans 3 model families × 3 decoding strategies, with scale consistency confirmed at 14B.\\n\\n3. **Cross-strategy depth estimation via two-stage pipeline + strategy-invariant signal discovery**: Naive cross-strategy transfer degrades to 59.1% with d1v2 boundary inversion. We propose a two-stage solution: (a) strategy identification classifier (57.8% — strategies share more feature space than expected), then (b) strategy-conditioned depth classifiers. The pipeline improves cross-strategy accuracy by **+11.5pp** on average (59.1% → 70.5%), with per-model results: GPT-2 XL 73.0%, OLMo 69.4%, Pythia 69.3%. Oracle ceiling at 78.3%. **Caveat**: improvement is consistent across models but absolute accuracy varies (2/3 models individually below 70%); paper must claim \\\"+11.5pp improvement\\\" rather than \\\"≥70% universal recovery\\\". The surprising finding: even with imperfect strategy routing, the pipeline works because depth signal has a **strategy-invariant core** (~60% accuracy floor) plus a strategy-specific component. This reframes cross-strategy transfer from \\\"broken\\\" to \\\"substantially improved with clear ceiling for future work\\\".\\n\\n4. **[Discussion — Practical Implication]** Depth-graduated curation as alternative to binary filtering: predicted depth provides a finer-grained quality proxy than binary real/synthetic labels, enabling graduated weighting strategies. Conceptual argument supported by depth-accuracy correlation in 3×3 matrix data; full experimental validation deferred to future work (D012).\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: Web-scraped training corpora increasingly contain multi-generational synthetic content from uncontrolled model→data→model feedback loops. Existing detectors answer \\\"is this synthetic?\\\" but not \\\"how many recursive cycles has this undergone?\\\" — a critical distinction since 1st-gen synthetic data can improve training while deeply recursive text drives distribution collapse.\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Binary detectors (LOKI, DivEye, Binoculars) classify real vs. synthetic but discard ordinal depth information. EditLens estimates single-pass AI editing extent but not recursive depth. Model collapse studies (Drayson et al. EMNLP 2025; Shumailov et al. Nature 2024) characterize degradation patterns but assume depth is known a priori — no method estimates depth from text alone.\\n\\n- **Our framing**: We formalize ordinal generation depth estimation as a new task, construct a multi-generational benchmark spanning 1B–14B models across 3 decoding strategies and 4 depths, and provide the first **Depth Degradation Atlas** — a systematic empirical map of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive depths, models, and strategies. The Atlas reveals (a) perplexity tail statistics as universal depth markers across all settings, (b) model-dependent strategy interaction effects with an identified root cause, and (c) the Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable. We show that feature-based classifiers achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy, and propose a two-stage strategy-aware pipeline that solves the cross-strategy deployment challenge. The depth signal persists under prompted generation (not just BOS-seeded), confirming ecological relevance.\\n\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\"On our multi-generational benchmark spanning model scales from 1B to 14B and 3 decoding strategies, feature-based ordinal depth estimation achieves 70–85% accuracy (chance=25%), with perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) consistently among the top-4 features across all settings. The depth signal persists under prompted (topic-seeded) generation and at 14B scale without quantization. The Depth Degradation Atlas reveals that effective discriminable depth extends to depth 3 in 7/9 settings (T=0.75). We identify strategy-dependent feature trajectory reversal and propose a two-stage pipeline that restores near within-strategy accuracy for cross-strategy deployment.\\\"\\n- **Note**: 4-class (depth 0-3) is the confirmed setting. 6-class (depth 0-5) completed via plan_011.\\n\\n## DCOL Status — Contingency Activated (D010/D011, 2026-05-15)\\n\\nDCOL v1–v5 全部失败（embedding collapse / OOM / catastrophic forgetting / 超参违规 / NaN 梯度爆炸）。\\n\\n- **Claim 2 (Atlas/EDD) 升为 PRIMARY contribution**\\n- **Claim 1**: Feature-based methods (RF/CORAL/DivEye) 作为 depth estimation 方法，不依赖 DCOL\\n- **DCOL v6 (RoBERTa-large)**: 非阻塞尝试。成功 → Section 5 bonus；失败 → negative analysis section（\\\"contrastive ordinal learning 在文本深度估计的固有局限\\\"）\\n- **Paper type**: Task formalization + systematic empirical study + practical framework（非 method paper）\\n- Atlas + EDD + feature universality + model-strategy interaction = 充分支撑 EMNLP \\\"New Missions\\\" track\\n\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\n\\n- **\\\"Just an empiric\\n\\n... On our 3-model × 3-strategy benchmark (1–1.5B), Random Forest classifiers on 19 statistical features achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy (chance=25%), with the best configuration (GPT-2 XL, temp τ=0.9) reaching 85.4%. Feature-based methods (RF, CORAL, DivEye) consistently outperform single-feature baselines across all settings. **Scale robustness**: Clean FP16 13B experiment (Qwen2.5-14B, no quantization) confirms depth signal persists at mainstream model scale, addressing the \\\\\\\"small model only\\\\\\\" concern from prior 7B QLoRA experiments. **Ecological validity**: Prompted generation experiment (topic-seeded text, not BOS-only unconditional) confirms depth signal is not an artifact of unnatural generation mode.\\\\n\\\\n2. **Depth Degradation Atlas — systematic characterization of recursive collapse** (PRIMARY): We provide the first systematic empirical characterization of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive generation depths, revealing (a) perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl) and surprisal variance (var_surprisal) as the dominant depth signal family — consistently occupying the top-4 features across all models and strategies, though their relative ranking is model-dependent, (b) non-uniform inter-depth distances (depth 0→1 gap >> depth 2→3 gap), (c) an Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable, and (d) **strategy-dependent feature trajectory reversal** — temperature sampling causes the depth-1→depth-2 feature boundary to invert relative to nucleus/top-k (cross-strategy LOO d1v2 AUC as low as 0.13), demonstrating that decoding strategy fundamentally shapes the degradation trajectory rather than merely attenuating it, and (e) **accelerated convergence mechanism** — root cause analysis confirms a genuine structural distributional shift: temperature's uniform logit scaling (vs nucleus/top-k truncation) accelerates convergence to the attractor distribution, so temp09 depth N features ≈ nucleus/topk50 depth N+1 (GPT-2 XL temp09 d1 p90_ppl median=92.2, falls into nucleus/topk50 d2 range 65–92). This is not a simple scale artifact fixable by normalization — z-score normalization improves overall LR cross-strategy accuracy (25%→61%) but does **not** recover pairwise d1v2 AUC (GPT-2 XL 0.50→0.24, OLMo 0.50→0.19), and does **not** improve tree-based classifiers (RF Δ<0.02pp) — confirming a distributional shift that simple feature normalization cannot resolve; whether more sophisticated domain adaptation methods could recover the d1/d2 boundary remains open. This atlas spans 3 model families × 3 decoding strategies, with scale consistency confirmed at 14B.\\\\n\\\\n3. **Cross-strategy depth estimation via two-stage pipeline + strategy-invariant signal discovery**: Naive cross-strategy transfer degrades to 59.1% with d1v2 boundary inversion. We propose a two-stage solution: (a) strategy identification classifier (57.8% — strategies share more feature space than expected), then (b) strategy-conditioned depth classifiers. The pipeline improves cross-strategy accuracy by **+11.5pp** on average (59.1% → 70.5%), with per-model results: GPT-2 XL 73.0%, OLMo 69.4%, Pythia 69.3%. Oracle ceiling at 78.3%. **Caveat**: improvement is consistent across models but absolute accuracy varies (2/3 models individually below 70%); paper must claim \\\\\\\"+11.5pp improvement\\\\\\\" rather than \\\\\\\"≥70% universal recovery\\\\\\\". The surprising finding: even with imperfect strategy routing, the pipeline works because depth signal has a **strategy-invariant core** (~60% accuracy floor) plus a strategy-specific component. This reframes cross-strategy transfer from \\\\\\\"broken\\\\\\\" to \\\\\\\"substantially improved with clear ceiling for future work\\\\\\\".\\\\n\\\\n4. **[Discussion — Practical Implication]** Depth-graduated curation as alternative to binary filtering: predicted depth provides a finer-grained quality proxy than binary real/synthetic labels, enabling graduated weighting strategies. Conceptual argument supported by depth-accuracy correlation in 3×3 matrix data; full experimental validation deferred to future work (D012).\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: Web-scraped training corpora increasingly contain multi-generational synthetic content from uncontrolled model→data→model feedback loops. **GPT-2 XL d3 checkpoint**: `checkpoints/gpt2xl/lora_depth_2/` (用于生成 d3；尚无 lora_depth_3，需先 finetune d3 数据)\\n- **d0-d3 数据**: `data/gpt2xl/depth_{0,1,2,3}.jsonl` (各 5000 samples)\\n- **d0-d3 特征**: `data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl` (20000 samples, evaluator=GPT-2 XL)\\n- **Pipeline 脚本**: `scripts/run_depth_extension_gpt2xl.sh`\\n- **模型路径**: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl`\\n- **LoRA config**: r=16, alpha=32, dropout=0.05, target=c_attn,c_proj\\n\\n## Pipeline 步骤\\n1. finetune d3 → lora_depth_3 (如已存在则跳过)\\n2. generate d4 (5000 samples, nucleus p=0.95, seed=42)\\n3. finetune d4 → lora_depth_4\\n4. inversion in RF — RF is scale-invariant (tree splits unaffected by monotonic transforms). Prior z-score recovery (0.13→0.68) was with LR, not RF.\"}, \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Z-score standardization has negligible effect on RF cross-strategy LOO. temp09 d1v2 AUC remains inverted: GPT-2 XL 0.1288→0.1348, OLMo 0.2529→0.2497, Pythia 0.576→0.594. Root cause: RF is a tree-based model invariant to monotonic feature transforms — z-score shifts/scales features but preserves relative ordering, so split thresholds adapt automatically. Prior independent verification (GPT-2 XL 0.13→0.68, OLMo 0.25→0.83) was with a scale-sensitive classifier (LR), not RF. Raw results match cross_strategy_loo_001 exactly (mean acc=0.5908). Non-temp09 impact negligible (mean Δd1v2=+0.006). Conclusion: covariate shift hypothesis is correct but the z-score fix is classifier-dependent — works for LR, not for RF.\"}, \"baseline_battery_9cell_001\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T04:26:41.388Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T05:11:25.784Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"9-cell baseline battery: 4 种分类方法 (RF/LR/CORAL/DivEye) 在 3×3 矩阵全部 cells 上的 4-class accuracy 对比，生成 paper Table 1\", \"parent\": \"baseline_battery_001\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"metrics\": {\"best_acc_matrix\": {\"pythia_nucleus\": 0.782, \"pythia_temp09\": 0.772, \"pythia_topk50\": 0.688, \"olmo_nucleus\": 0.796, \"olmo_temp09\": 0.827, \"olmo_topk50\": 0.759, \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": 0.843, \"gpt2xl_temp09\": 0.853, \"gpt2xl_topk50\": 0.754}, \"methods\": [\"RF\", \"LR\", \"CORAL\", \"DivEye\"], \"cells\": 9, \"finding\": \"RF ≈ CORAL > DivEye > LR, max gap ~4pp. GPT-2 XL temp09 best (85.3%), model effect > strategy effect\", \"WARNING\": \"best_acc_matrix stores best-across-ALL-methods accuracy, NOT RF-only.
